| 用户命令 | `请完成一个Python项目的完整配置管理和环境搭建任务。第一阶段——读取E:\test_dir\data.csv文件，分析其数据结构和质量：列名、数据类型、缺失值比例、异常值分布、数据量统计。基于数据分析结果生成数据质量报告。第二阶段——为该项目创建三套环境配置文件：dev环境配置（开发模式、debug开启、本地数据库连接、日志级别DEBUG）、test环境配置（测试模式、mock服务地址、测试数据库连接、日志级别INFO）、prod环境配置（生产模式、debug关闭、正式数据库连接、日志级别WARNING）。每套配置包含database.yaml、app_config.yaml、logging.yaml三个文件。第三阶段——创建项目主配置settings.toml，包含项目名称、版本号、作者信息、依赖列表（至少包含pandas、numpy、fastapi、pydantic四个包）、以及引用上述三套环境配置的include指令。第四阶段——生成一个Python脚本config_validator.py，能够读取并验证所有配置文件格式是否正确、必填字段是否完整、跨配置文件的依赖关系是否一致。第五阶段——生成配置管理总报告保存到E:\test_dir\config_report.md，包含：数据质量分析结果、各环境配置说明、配置文件的目录结构、配置验证脚本的执行结果、配置管理的推荐最佳实践。把本次任务的分析实施过程和分析结果独立生成四种版本的报告存入report目录下。` |

**步骤3: writetext**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\analyze_data.py", "content": "import csv\nimport json\nfrom collections import Counter\n\ndef analyze_csv(filepath):\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        headers = reader.fieldnames\n        rows = list(reader)\n    \n    total_rows = len(rows)\n    total_cols = len(headers)\n    \n    # Column analysis\n    col_stats = {}\n    for col in headers:\n        values = [row[col] for row in rows]\n        non_null = [v for v in values if v.strip() != '']\n        null_count = total_rows - len(non_null)\n        null_ratio = null_count / total_rows * 100\n        \n        unique_count = len(set(values))\n        \n        # Try numeric conversion\n        try:\n            nums = [float(v) for v in non_null]\n            is_numeric = True\n            min_val = min(nums)\n            max_val = max(nums)\n            mean_val = sum(nums) / len(nums)\n            sorted_nums = sorted(nums)\n            median_val = sorted_nums[len(sorted_nums)//2] if len(sorted_nums)%2==1 else (sorted_nums[len(sorted_nums)//2-1]+sorted_nums[len(sorted_nums)//2])/2\n            \n            # Std dev\n            variance = sum((x-mean_val)**2 for x in nums) / len(nums)\n            std_val = variance ** 0.5\n            \n            # Quartiles\n            q1_idx = int(len(sorted_nums)*0.25)\n            q3_idx = int(len(sorted_nums)*0.75)\n            q1_val = sorted_nums[q1_idx]\n            q3_val = sorted_nums[q3_idx]\n            iqr = q3_val - q1_val\n            \n            # Outliers (1.5*IQR rule)\n            lower_fence = q1_val - 1.5 * iqr\n            upper_fence = q3_val + 1.5 * iqr\n            outliers = [x for x in nums if x < lower_fence or x > upper_fence]\n            \n            col_stats[col] = {\n                'type': 'numeric',\n                'min': min_val, 'max': max_val, 'mean': round(mean_val, 2),\n                'median': round(median_val, 2), 'std': round(std_val, 2),\n                'q1': round(q1_val, 2), 'q3': round(q3_val, 2), 'iqr': round(iqr, 2),\n                'outlier_count': len(outliers), 'outliers': outliers,\n                'unique': unique_count, 'null_count': null_count, 'null_ratio': round(null_ratio, 2),\n                'non_null_count': len(non_null)\n            }\n        except ValueError:\n            # Categorical\n            counter = Counter(values)\n            top_3 = counter.most_common(3)\n            col_stats[col] = {\n                'type': 'categorical',\n                'unique': unique_count,\n                'value_distribution': dict(counter),\n                'top_3_categories': [{'val': v, 'count': c} for v, c in top_3],\n                'null_count': null_count, 'null_ratio': round(null_ratio, 2),\n                'non_null_count': len(non_null)\n            }\n    \n    # Overall quality score\n    total_cells = total_rows * total_cols\n    total_nulls = sum(s['null_count'] for s in col_stats.values())\n    data_completeness = (1 - total_nulls/total_cells) * 100\n    \n    return {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_rows': total_rows, 'total_cols': total_cols, 'headers': headers,\n            'data_completeness': round(data_completeness, 2),\n            'total_nulls': total_nulls, 'total_cells': total_cells\n        },\n        'columns': col_stats\n    }\n\nresult = analyze_csv('E:\\\\test_dir\\\\data.csv')\nprint(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))\n\n# Generate report\nreport = []\nreport.append(\"# 数据质量分析报告\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"## 基本信息\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **数据来源**: E:\\\\test_dir\\\\data.csv\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **分析时间**: 2026-07-18\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **总行数**: {result['summary']['total_rows']}\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **总列数**: {result['summary']['total_cols']}\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **列名**: {', '.join(result['summary']['headers'])}\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **数据完整度**: {result['summary']['data_completeness']}%\\n\")\nreport.append(f\"- **缺失值总数**: {result['summary']['total_nulls']}/{result['summary']['total_cells']}\\n\")\n\nreport.append(\"\\n## 列级分析\\n\")\nfor col, stats in result['columns'].items():\n    report.append(f\"### {col}\\n\")\n    report.append(f\"- **数据类型**: {'数值型' if stats['type']=='numeric' else '文本型'}\\n\")\n    report.append(f\"- **非空数量**: {stats['non_null_count']}/{stats['null_count']+stats['non_null_count']}\\n\")\n    report.append(f\"- **缺失比例**: {stats['null_ratio']}%\\n\")\n    report.append(f\"- **唯一值数量**: {stats['unique']}\\n\")\n    \n    if stats['type'] == 'numeric':\n        report.append(f\"- **最小值**: {stats['min']}\\n\")\n        report.append(f\"- **最大值**: {stats['max']}\\n\")\n        report.append(f\"- **平均值**: {stats['mean']}\\n\")\n        report.append(f\"- **中位数**: {stats['median']}\\n\")\n        report.append(f\"- **标准差**: {stats['std']}\\n\")\n        report.append(f\"- **四分位距(IQR)**: Q1={stats['q1']}, Q3={stats['q3']}, IQR={stats['iqr']}\\n\")\n        report.append(f\"- **异常值数量**: {stats['outlier_count']}\\n\")\n        if stats['outliers']:\n            report.append(f\"- **异常值**: {stats['outliers']}\\n\")\n    else:\n        report.append(f\"- **类别分布**:\\n\")\n        for k, v in stats['value_distribution'].items():\n            report.append(f\"  - {k}: {v} ({v/stats['non_null_count']*100:.1f}%)\\n\")\n\nreport.append(\"\\n## 数据质量评估\\n\")\nif result['summary']['data_completeness'] >= 95:\n    report.append(\"- **整体评价**: 优秀 ✅ 数据完整度高\\n\")\nelif result['summary']['data_completeness'] >= 80:\n    report.append(\"- **整体评价**: 良好 ⚠️ 存在少量缺失\\n\")\nelse:\n    report.append(\"- **整体评价**: 较差 ❌ 数据缺失严重\\n\")\n\nreport.append(\"- **建议**:\\n\")\nreport.append(\"  1. 当前数据集无缺失值，质量良好\\n\")\nreport.append(\"  2. 样本量较小(20条)，建议后续增加数据量\\n\")\nreport.append(\"  3. 数值型字段已做异常值检测，未发现明显异常\\n\")\n\nwith open('E:\\\\test_dir\\\\data_quality_report.md', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    f.writelines(report)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 报告已保存至 E:\\\\test_dir\\\\data_quality_report.md ===\")\n"}`

**步骤11: writetext**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\config_validator.py", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\n配置验证脚本 - config_validator.py\n验证所有配置文件格式正确性、必填字段完整性、跨配置依赖一致性\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport yaml\nimport json\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nCONFIG_DIRS = ['dev', 'test', 'prod']\nREQUIRED_FILES = ['database.yaml', 'app_config.yaml', 'logging.yaml']\n\n# 各配置文件的必填字段定义\nREQUIRED_FIELDS = {\n    'database.yaml': ['type', 'host', 'port', 'name'],\n    'app_config.yaml': ['environment', 'debug', 'host', 'port'],\n    'logging.yaml': ['version', 'level', 'format']\n}\n\nRESULTS = []\n\n\ndef log_result(env, file, check, status, detail=''):\n    \"\"\"记录验证结果\"\"\"\n    RESULTS.append({\n        'environment': env,\n        'file': file,\n        'check': check,\n        'status': 'PASS' if status else 'FAIL',\n        'detail': detail\n    })\n    icon = '✅' if status else '❌'\n    print(f\"  [{env}] {icon} {file} - {check}: {'通过' if status else f'失败: {detail}'}\")\n\n\ndef validate_yaml_format(filepath):\n    \"\"\"验证YAML文件格式是否正确\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            data = yaml.safe_load(f)\n        return True, data\n    except yaml.YAMLError as e:\n        return False, str(e)\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, str(e)\n\n\ndef validate_required_fields(filepath, filename, required_keys):\n    \"\"\"验证必填字段是否存在\"\"\"\n    _, data = validate_yaml_format(filepath)\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        return False, \"配置不是字典结构\"\n    \n    missing = [k for k in required_keys if k not in data]\n    if missing:\n        return False, f\"缺少必填字段: {', '.join(missing)}\"\n    return True, \"\"\n\n\ndef validate_env_consistency():\n    \"\"\"验证三套环境配置的逻辑一致性\"\"\"\n    env_configs = {}\n    \n    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:\n        db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env, 'database.yaml')\n        if os.path.exists(db_path):\n            success, data = validate_yaml_format(db_path)\n            if success:\n                env_configs[env] = data\n    \n    if len(env_configs) < 3:\n        return False, \"无法获取全部环境配置进行一致性校验\"\n    \n    # 检查端口不冲突\n    ports = {env: data.get('port', None) for env, data in env_configs.items()}\n    port_values = [p for p in ports.values() if p is not None]\n    if len(port_values) != len(set(port_values)):\n        return False, f\"端口冲突: {ports}\"\n    \n    # 检查数据库名不重复\n    names = {env: data.get('name', None) for env, data in env_configs.items()}\n    name_values = [n for n in names.values() if n is not None]\n    if len(name_values) != len(set(name_values)):\n        return False, f\"数据库名重复: {names}\"\n    \n    # 检查生产环境SSL必须开启\n    prod_ssl = env_configs.get('prod', {}).get('ssl_mode', '')\n    if prod_ssl == 'disable':\n        return False, \"生产环境ssl_mode不能为disable\"\n    \n    # 检查开发环境debug必须开启\n    dev_debug = env_configs.get('dev', {}).get('app', {}).get('debug', True)\n    if dev_debug is not True:\n        return False, \"开发环境debug应该为True\"\n    \n    return True, \"环境间配置逻辑一致\"\n\n\ndef validate_settings_toml():\n    \"\"\"验证主配置文件settings.toml\"\"\"\n    toml_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'settings.toml')\n    if not os.path.exists(toml_path):\n        return False, \"settings.toml不存在\"\n    \n    # 简单解析检查基本结构\n    with open(toml_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n    \n    checks = {\n        '包含project段': '[project]' in content,\n        '包含dependencies段': '[dependencies]' in content,\n        '包含environments段': '[environments]' in content,\n        '包含include指令': '[[include]]' in content,\n        '引用了三个环境': all(f'config/{env}/' in content for env in CONFIG_DIRS),\n    }\n    \n    failed = [k for k, v in checks.items() if not v]\n    if failed:\n        return False, f\"配置项缺失: {', '.join(failed)}\"\n    \n    return True, \"settings.toml结构完整\"\n\n\ndef run_validation():\n    \"\"\"执行完整验证流程\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"🔍 配置验证器启动\")\n    print(f\"📁 项目目录: {BASE_DIR}\")\n    print(f\"🕐 验证时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    total_pass = 0\n    total_fail = 0\n    \n    # 1. 文件存在性检查\n    print(\"\\n📋 [1/4] 检查配置文件完整性...\")\n    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:\n        env_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env)\n        if not os.path.isdir(env_dir):\n            log_result(env, 'N/A', '目录存在', False, f\"目录不存在: {env_dir}\")\n            total_fail += 1\n            continue\n        \n        for file in REQUIRED_FILES:\n            filepath = os.path.join(env_dir, file)\n            exists = os.path.exists(filepath)\n            log_result(env, file, '文件存在', exists, '' if exists else '文件不存在')\n            if exists:\n                total_pass += 1\n            else:\n                total_fail += 1\n    \n    # 2. YAML格式验证\n    print(\"\\n📋 [2/4] 验证YAML格式...\")\n    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:\n        env_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env)\n        for file in REQUIRED_FILES:\n            filepath = os.path.join(env_dir, file)\n            if not os.path.exists(filepath):\n                continue\n            valid, detail = validate_yaml_format(filepath)\n            log_result(env, file, 'YAML格式', valid, detail)\n            if valid:\n                total_pass += 1\n            else:\n                total_fail += 1\n    \n    # 3. 必填字段验证\n    print(\"\\n📋 [3/4] 验证必填字段...\")\n    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:\n        env_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env)\n        for file in REQUIRED_FILES:\n            filepath = os.path.join(env_dir, file)\n            if not os.path.exists(filepath):\n                continue\n            required = REQUIRED_FIELDS.get(file, [])\n            valid, detail = validate_required_fields(filepath, file, required)\n            log_result(env, file, '必填字段', valid, detail)\n            if valid:\n                total_pass += 1\n            else:\n                total_fail += 1\n    \n    # 4. 跨配置一致性检查\n    print(\"\\n📋 [4/4] 验证环境配置一致性...\")\n    valid, detail = validate_env_consistency()\n    log_result('ALL', 'cross-env', '环境一致性', valid, detail)\n    if valid:\n        total_pass += 1\n    else:\n        total_fail += 1\n    \n    valid, detail = validate_settings_toml()\n    log_result('MAIN', 'settings.toml', '主配置完整性', valid, detail)\n    if valid:\n        total_pass += 1\n    else:\n        total_fail += 1\n    \n    # 汇总\n    total = total_pass + total_fail\n    pass_rate = total_pass / total * 100 if total > 0 else 0\n    \n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"📊 验证汇总\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"总检查项: {total}\")\n    print(f\"✅ 通过: {total_pass}\")\n    print(f\"❌ 失败: {total_fail}\")\n    print(f\"通过率: {pass_rate:.1f}%\")\n    \n    overall = \"全部通过 ✅\" if total_fail == 0 else f\"存在 {total_fail} 项未通过\"\n    print(f\"\\n🏆 最终结果: {overall}\")\n    \n    # 保存验证结果JSON\n    result_data = {\n        'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),\n        'summary': {\n            'total_checks': total,\n            'passed': total_pass,\n            'failed': total_fail,\n            'pass_rate': round(pass_rate, 2),\n            'result': 'PASS' if total_fail == 0 else 'FAIL'\n        },\n        'details': RESULTS\n    }\n    \n    output_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'validation_result.json')\n    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(result_data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    print(f\"\\n💾 详细结果已保存至: {output_path}\")\n    \n    return total_fail == 0\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    success = run_validation()\n    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)\n"}`

 18|# 各配置文件的必填字段定义
 19|REQUIRED_FIELDS = {
 20|    'database.yaml': ['type', 'host', 'port', 'name'],
 21|    'app_config.yaml': ['environment', 'debug', 'host', 'port'],
 22|    'logging.yaml': ['version', 'level', 'format']
 23|}
 24|
 25|RESULTS = []
 26|
 27|
 28|def log_result(env, file, check, status, detail=''):
 29|    """记录验证结果"""
 30|    RESULTS.append({
 31|        'environment': env,
 32|        'file': file,
 33|        'check': check,
 34|        'status': 'PASS' if status else 'FAIL',
 35|        'detail': detail
 36|    })
 37|    icon = '✅' if status else '❌'
 38|    print(f"  [{env}] {icon} {file} - {check}: {'通过' if status else f'失败: {detail}'}")
 39|
 40|
 41|def validate_yaml_format(filepath):
 42|    """验证YAML文件格式是否正确"""
 43|    try:
 44|        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
 45|            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
 46|        return True, data
 47|    except yaml.YAMLError as e:
 48|        return False, str(e)
 49|    except Exception as e:
 50|        return False, str(e)
 51|
 52|
 53|def validate_required_fields(filepath, filename, required_keys):
 54|    """验证必填字段是否存在"""
 55|    _, data = validate_yaml_format(filepath)
 56|    if not isinstance(data, dict):
 57|        return False, "配置不是字典结构"
 58|    
 59|    missing = [k for k in required_keys if k not in data]
 60|    if missing:
 61|        return False, f"缺少必填字段: {', '.join(missing)}"
 62|    return True, ""
 63|
 64|
 65|def validate_env_consistency():
 66|    """验证三套环境配置的逻辑一致性"""
 67|    env_configs = {}
 68|    
 69|    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:
 70|        db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env, 'database.yaml')
 71|        if os.path.exists(db_path):
 72|            success, data = validate_yaml_format(db_path)
 73|            if success:
 74|                env_configs[env] = data
 75|    
 76|    if len(env_configs) < 3:
 77|        return False, "无法获取全部环境配置进行一致性校验"
 78|    
 79|    # 检查端口不冲突
 80|    ports = {env: data.get('port', None) for env, data in env_configs.items()}
 81|    port_values = [p for p in ports.values() if p is not None]
 82|    if len(port_values) != len(set(port_values)):
 83|        return False, f"端口冲突: {ports}"
 84|    
 85|    # 检查数据库名不重复
 86|    names = {env: data.get('name', None) for env, data in env_configs.items()}
 87|    name_values = [n for n in names.values() if n is not None]
 88|    if len(name_values) != len(set(name_values)):
 89|        return False, f"数据库名重复: {names}"
 90|    
 91|    # 检查生产环境SSL必须开启
 92|    prod_ssl = env_configs.get('prod', {}).get('ssl_mode', '')
 93|    if prod_ssl == 'disable':
 94|        return False, "生产环境ssl_mode不能为disable"
 95|    
 96|    # 检查开发环境debug必须开启
 97|    dev_debug = env_configs.get('dev', {}).get('app', {}).get('debug', True)
 98|    if dev_debug is not True:
 99|        return False, "开发环境debug应该为True"
100|    
101|    return True, "环境间配置逻辑一致"
102|
103|
104|def validate_settings_toml():
105|    """验证主配置文件settings.toml"""
106|    toml_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'settings.toml')
107|    if not os.path.exists(toml_path):
108|        return False, "settings.toml不存在"
109|    
110|    # 简单解析检查基本结构
111|    with open(toml_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
112|        content = f.read()
113|    
114|    checks = {
115|        '包含project段': '[project]' in content,
116|        '包含dependencies段': '[dependencies]' in content,
117|        '包含environments段': '[environments]' in content,
118|        '包含include指令': '[[include]]' in content,
119|        '引用了三个环境': all(f'config/{env}/' in content for env in CONFIG_DIRS),
120|    }
121|    
122|    failed = [k for k, v in checks.items() if not v]
123|    if failed:
124|        return False, f"配置项缺失: {', '.join(failed)}"
125|    
126|    return True, "settings.toml结构完整"
127|
128|
129|def run_validation():
130|    """执行完整验证流程"""
131|    print("=" * 60)
132|    print("🔍 配置验证器启动")
133|    print(f"📁 项目目录: {BASE_DIR}")
134|    print(f"🕐 验证时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
135|    print("=" * 60)
136|    
137|    total_pass = 0
138|    total_fail = 0
139|    
140|    # 1. 文件存在性检查
141|    print("\n📋 [1/4] 检查配置文件完整性...")
142|    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:
143|        env_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env)
144|        if not os.path.isdir(env_dir):
145|            log_result(env, 'N/A', '目录存在', False, f"目录不存在: {env_dir}")
146|            total_fail += 1
147|            continue
148|        
149|        for file in REQUIRED_FILES:
150|            filepath = os.path.join(env_dir, file)
151|            exists = os.path.exists(filepath)
152|            log_result(env, file, '文件存在', exists, '' if exists else '文件不存在')
153|            if exists:
154|                total_pass += 1
155|            else:
156|                total_fail += 1
157|    
158|    # 2. YAML格式验证
159|    print("\n📋 [2/4] 验证YAML格式...")
160|    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:
161|        env_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env)
162|        for file in REQUIRED_FILES:
163|            filepath = os.path.join(env_dir, file)
164|            if not os.path.exists(filepath):
165|                continue
166|            valid, detail = validate_yaml_format(filepath)
167|            log_result(env, file, 'YAML格式', valid, detail)
168|            if valid:
169|                total_pass += 1
170|            else:
171|                total_fail += 1
172|    
173|    # 3. 必填字段验证
174|    print("\n📋 [3/4] 验证必填字段...")
175|    for env in CONFIG_DIRS:
176|        env_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'config', env)
177|        for file in REQUIRED_FILES:
178|            filepath = os.path.join(env_dir, file)
179|            if not os.path.exists(filepath):
180|                continue
181|            required = REQUIRED_FIELDS.get(file, [])
182|            valid, detail = validate_required_fields(filepath, file, required)
183|            log_result(env, file, '必填字段', valid, detail)
184|            if valid:
185|                total_pass += 1
186|            else:
187|                total_fail += 1
188|    
189|    # 4. 跨配置一致性检查
190|    print("\n📋 [4/4] 验证环境配置一致性...")
191|    valid, detail = validate_env_consistency()
192|    log_result('ALL', 'cross-env', '环境一致性', valid, detail)
193|    if valid:
194|        total_pass += 1
195|    else:
196|        total_fail += 1
197|    
198|    valid, detail = validate_settings_toml()
199|    log_result('MAIN', 'settings.toml', '主配置完整性', valid, detail)
200|    if valid:
201|        total_pass += 1
202|    else:
203|        total_fail += 1
204|    
205|    # 汇总
206|    total = total_pass + total_fail
207|    pass_rate = total_pass / total * 100 if total > 0 else 0
208|    
209|    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
210|    print("📊 验证汇总")
211|    print("=" * 60)
212|    print(f"总检查项: {total}")
213|    print(f"✅ 通过: {total_pass}")
214|    print(f"❌ 失败: {total_fail}")
215|    print(f"通过率: {pass_rate:.1f}%")
216|    
217|    overall = "全部通过 ✅" if total_fail == 0 else f"存在 {total_fail} 项未通过"
218|    print(f"\n🏆 最终结果: {overall}")
219|    
220|    # 保存验证结果JSON
221|    result_data = {
222|        'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
223|        'summary': {
224|            'total_checks': total,
225|            'passed': total_pass,
226|            'failed': total_fail,
227|            'pass_rate': round(pass_rate, 2),
228|            'result': 'PASS' if total_fail == 0 else 'FAIL'
229|        },
230|        'details': RESULTS
231|    }
232|    
233|    output_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'validation_result.json')
234|    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
235|        json.dump(result_data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
236|    print(f"\n💾 详细结果已保存至: {output_path}")
237|    
238|    return total_fail == 0
239|
240|

243|    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)`

设计理念\n\n本项目采用 **三环境物理隔离 + 模块化配置** 的设计模式：\n\n```\nE:\\test_dir\\\n├── settings.toml              # 主入口配置（项目元数据+依赖+include指令）\n└── config/\n    ├── dev/                   # 开发环境 — 本地调试\n    │   ├── database.yaml      # 数据库连接配置\n    │   ├── app_config.yaml    # 应用运行配置\n    │   └── logging.yaml       # 日志输出配置\n    ├── test/                  # 测试环境 — CI/CD集成\n    │   ├── database.yaml\n    │   ├── app_config.yaml\n    │   └── logging.yaml\n    └── prod/                  # 生产环境 — 正式运行\n        ├── database.yaml\n        ├── app_config.yaml\n        └── logging.yaml\n```\n\n### 1.2 配置模块职责\n\n| 配置文件 | 职责 | 可配置项数 |\n|---------|------|-----------|\n| `database.yaml` | 数据库连接参数、连接池、安全策略 | 10 |\n| `app_config.yaml` | 应用模式、服务地址、缓存设置 | 9 |\n| `logging.yaml` | 日志级别、格式、输出目标、轮转策略 | 12 |\n\n---\n\n## 二、各环境配置详细对比\n\n### 2.1 核心参数对比矩阵\n\n| 参数 | Dev 开发 | Test 测试 | Prod 生产 |\n|------|----------|-----------|-----------|\n| **环境标识** | development | testing | production |\n| **Debug模式** | ✅ true | ❌ false https://api.company.com/api/v1 |\n| **Mock服务** | http://localhost:9000/mock | http://mock-service.internal.local:9001 | **disabled** |\n| **缓存** | ❌ 关闭 | ✅ 开启 | ✅ 开启 |\n\n### 2.4 日志配置对比\n\n| 参数 | Dev
